# Complete file guide

Catalogue generated from the Git index plus the explicitly named handoff documents/utility in this delivery. Feature evidence remains the dated validation report; a catalogue refresh does not rerun tests.

**Coverage:** 1,574 project paths. Every current tracked path appears exactly once below. The scope excludes `.git` internals and ignored dependency/build bodies; those are recorded separately in [the local generated-file inventory](../../AnalyseFuture/repository-inventory/LOCAL_GENERATED_FILE_INVENTORY.txt).

This describes responsibility, not a claim that each feature runs. Read [the root handoff](../../PROJECT_HANDOFF.md), [folder guide](FOLDER_GUIDE.md), and [current validation report](../../AnalyseFuture/VALIDATION_REPORT.txt) for status and tasks. Primary app source is under `apps`; `AnalyseFuture` originals/references are excluded from production execution. Binary assets are identified by their consumer/metadata rather than asserted decoded or tested.

The dependency evidence is in [the static relationship map](../../AnalyseFuture/repository-inventory/dependency-relationships.json). Imported/declaration relationships do not prove runtime call behavior. Historical originals retain their former role but are not current setup instructions.

## .

| File | Responsibility |
| --- | --- |
| [.dockerignore](../../.dockerignore) | Exclude archives, preserved references and caches from root Docker build contexts. |
| [.gitignore](../../.gitignore) | Keep dependencies, caches and generated outputs out of versioned source. |
| [AGENTS.md](../../AGENTS.md) | Owner scope, preservation and contributor delivery instructions. |
| [PROJECT_HANDOFF.md](../../PROJECT_HANDOFF.md) | Current goals, verified status, detailed tasks and external-tool PR review contract. |
| [PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md](../../PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md) | Functional ownership map and reasons framework files stay together. |
| [README.md](../../README.md) | Homepage overview, current validation entry points and navigation. |
